. The dotted lines represent the level curve
\mathrm{Re}h(w)=\mathrm{Re}h(w_{c})
. It consists of two closed contours and one infinite contour all of which pass the critical point
w_{c}
. The complex plane thus is split into four parts, two of them marked with
-
signs have lower levels of
\mathrm{Re}h(w)
, and the other two marked with
+
signs have higher levels of
\mathrm{Re}h(w)
. The three solid contours on the left, from inside to outside, are
\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}
,
\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}
,
\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}
respectively. The three solid contours on the right, from inside to outside, are
\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}
,
\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}
, and
\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}
respectively. Each contour
\Sigma_{*}
is split into two parts. The part within the dashed circle is
\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}
, and the remaining part is
\Sigma_{*}^{(\mathrm{err})}
.

Combining with the bounds of
f_{i}
at the endpoints of
\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}
discussed above, we have the following two estimates

\max_{u\in\Sigma_{*}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|f_{i}(u;t_{i})/f_{i}(w_{c};t_{i})|\leq\min_{u\in\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}}|f_{i}(u;t_{i})/f_{i}(w_{c};t_{i})|\leq e^{-c(\ln N)^{3}},*\in\{\{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}\},\{\mathrm{L}\},\{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}\}\},

(3.21)

\min_{v\in\Sigma_{*}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|f_{i}(v;t_{i})/f_{i}(w_{c};t_{i})|\geq\min_{v\in\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}}|f_{i}(v;t_{i})/f_{i}(w_{c};t_{i})|\geq e^{c(\ln N)^{3}},*\in\{\{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}\},\{\mathrm{R}\},\{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}\}\}.

(3.22)

We remark that the contours we choose above are independent of the parameters
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
, hence the constant
c
above is also independent of
k_{1}
and
k_{2}
.

With the contours we mentioned above, we can rewrite

\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)=\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)+\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N),

where

\begin{split}&\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)\\
&=\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(N)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}^{(N)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(N)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(N)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\cdot\left(1-\mathrm{z}\right)^{k_{2}}\left(1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right)^{k_{1}}\cdot\frac{f_{1}(U^{(1)};t_{1})f_{2}(U^{(2)};t_{2})}{f_{1}(V^{(1)};t_{1})f_{2}(V^{(2)};t_{2})}\cdot\frac{1}{\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\sum_{i_{\ell}=1}^{k_{\ell}}(u_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}-v_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)})}\\
&\quad\cdot H(U^{(1)},U^{(2)};V^{(1)},V^{(2)})\cdot\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{\left(\Delta(U^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}\left(\Delta(V^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}{\left(\Delta(U^{(\ell)};V^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Delta(U^{(1)};V^{(2)})\Delta(V^{(1)};U^{(2)})}{\Delta(U^{(1)};U^{(2)})\Delta(V^{(1)};V^{(2)})}.\end{split}

(3.23)

Note that
\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}
has the same formula as
\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}
in (
3.6
) except that we replace all the
\Sigma_{*}
contours to
\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}
. Recall that we have
\Sigma_{*}=\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}\cup\Sigma_{*}^{(\mathrm{err})}
. Hence

\begin{split}&\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)\\
&=\sum_{\Delta}\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\cdots\end{split}

(3.24)

where we did not write out the integrand which is the same as in (
3.23
), and the summation is over all possible
\Delta
’s each of which belongs to
\{N,\mathrm{err}\}
and at least one
\Delta
is
\mathrm{err}
. We also point out that we omit the indices of
\Delta
in
\Sigma_{*}^{(\Delta)}
: It indeed depends on the choice of
*
and
i_{1}
or
i_{2}
. Since we have
4k_{1}+2k_{2}
integration contours, we have
2^{4k_{1}+2k_{2}}-1
possible choices of
\Delta
in the above summation.

Similarly we can write

\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)=\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)+\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma),

where
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)
has the same formula as (
3.8
) with all the integration contours
\Gamma_{*}
replaced by
\Gamma_{*}^{(N)}
, and
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)
is a summation of
2^{4k_{1}+2k_{2}}-1
terms each of which has the same formula as (
3.8
) except that the integration contours are all replaced by
\Gamma_{*}^{(N)}
or
\Gamma^{(\mathrm{err})}_{*}
and at least one of the contours is replaced by
\Gamma^{(\mathrm{err})}_{*}
.

We will show the following two lemmas.

Lemma 3.5
.

With the same assumptions as in Proposition
3.1
, there exists a constant
\mathcal{C}_{2,1}
as described in Notation
3.2
, such that

\begin{split}&\left|\alpha^{-1/3}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{2/3}N^{2/3}\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)-\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)\right|\\
&\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2})/2}\mathcal{C}_{2,1}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{5}\end{split}

for all
k_{1},k_{2}\geq 1
as
N
becomes sufficiently large.

Lemma 3.6
.

With the same assumptions as in Proposition
3.1
, there exist two constants
\mathcal{C}_{2,3}
and
\mathcal{C}_{2,4}
as described in Notation
3.2
, such that

N^{2/3}\left|\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)\right|\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2})/2}\mathcal{C}_{2,3}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\cdot e^{-c\cdot(\ln N)^{3}/2},

and

\left|\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)\right|\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2})/2}\mathcal{C}_{2,4}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\cdot e^{-c\cdot(\ln N)^{3}/2}

for all
k_{1},k_{2}\geq 1
as
N
becomes sufficiently large. Here the constant
c
is the same as in (
3.21
) and (
3.22
).

It is obvious that Lemmas
3.4
follows immediately by the above lemmas. In the next two subsubsections we will prove Lemmas
3.5
and
3.6
respectively.

3.2.2
Proof of Lemma
3.5

We recall the formula (
3.23
) for
\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}
. We change the integration variables

\begin{split}u_{i_{1}}^{(1)}&=w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}N^{-1/3}\xi_{i_{1}}^{(1)},\\
v_{i_{1}}^{(1)}&=w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}N^{-1/3}\eta_{i_{1}}^{(1)},\\
u_{i_{2}}^{(2)}&=w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}N^{-1/3}\xi_{i_{2}}^{(2)},\\
v_{i_{2}}^{(2)}&=w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}N^{-1/3}\eta_{i_{2}}^{(2)},\end{split}

(3.25)

where
w_{c}=-(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-1}
is defined in (
3.16
),
\xi_{i_{1}}^{(1)}\in\Gamma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}\cup\Gamma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(N)}
,
\xi_{i_{2}}^{(2)}\in\Gamma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(N)}
,
\eta_{i_{1}}^{(1)}\in\Gamma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}\cup\Gamma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}^{(N)}
, and
\eta_{i_{2}}^{(2)}\in\Gamma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(N)}
. Note the relation between
\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}
contours and
\Gamma_{*}^{(N)}
contours in (
3.17
). Thus we have

\begin{split}&\alpha^{-1/3}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{2/3}N^{2/3}\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)\\
&=\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\xi^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\xi^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\eta^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\eta^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{L}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\xi^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{R}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\eta^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\cdot\left(1-\mathrm{z}\right)^{k_{2}}\left(1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right)^{k_{1}}\cdot\frac{\tilde{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};t_{1})\tilde{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};t_{2})}{\tilde{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};t_{1})\tilde{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)};t_{2})}\cdot\frac{1}{\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\sum_{i_{\ell}=1}^{k_{\ell}}(\xi_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}-\eta_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)})}\\
&\quad\cdot\tilde{H}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})\cdot\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{\left(\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}\left(\Delta(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}{\left(\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(\ell)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})\Delta(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)})}{\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)})\Delta(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})},\end{split}

(3.26)

where

\begin{split}&\tilde{f}_{\ell}(\xi_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)};t_{i})=f_{\ell}(u_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)};t_{i})/f_{\ell}(w_{c};t_{i}),\qquad\tilde{f}_{\ell}(\eta_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)};t_{i})=f_{\ell}(v_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)};t_{i})/f_{\ell}(w_{c};t_{i}),\\
&\tilde{H}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})=\alpha^{-2/3}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{10/3}N^{4/3}H(U^{(1)},U^{(2)};V^{(1)},V^{(2)})\end{split}

(3.27)

with the
u_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)},v_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}
being viewed as functions of
\xi_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}
and
\eta_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}
as in (
3.25
). Note that (
3.26
) equals to
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)
if we replace
\tilde{f}_{\ell}
by
\mathrm{f_{\ell}}
and
\tilde{H}
by
\mathrm{H}
, see (
3.8
) for the formula of
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}
and note that replacing the contours
\Gamma_{*}
by
\Gamma_{*}^{(N)}
in (
3.8
) gives the formula of
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{(k_{1},k_{2})}^{(N)}
.

Recall that
f_{1}(w;t_{1})=(w+1)^{-m}w^{n}e^{t_{1}w}
. Note the scaling in (
3.1
). For all
|\zeta|\leq\log N
, we have the following Taylor expansion

\begin{split}&\log\left(f_{1}(w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}\zeta N^{-1/3};t_{1})/f_{1}(w_{c};t_{1})\right)\\
&=-m\log\left(1+\alpha^{-1/3}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-1/3}\zeta N^{-1/3}\right)+n\log\left(1-\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-1/3}\zeta N^{-1/3}\right)+t_{1}\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}\zeta N^{-1/3}\\
&=-\frac{1}{3}\gamma\zeta^{3}-\frac{1}{2}({x}_{2}-{x}_{1})\zeta^{2}+\left(\mathrm{t}_{1}-\frac{1}{4\gamma}({x}_{2}-{x}_{1})^{2}\right)\zeta+\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4}),\end{split}

(3.28)

and hence, using the fact
e^{\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})}=1+\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})
,

\tilde{f}_{1}(w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}\zeta N^{-1/3};t_{1})=\mathrm{f}_{1}(\zeta;\mathrm{t}_{1})\cdot\left(1+\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})\right).

(3.29)

Note here the error term
\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})
is uniformly for all
|\zeta|\leq\log N
.
Similarly, for all
|\zeta|\leq\log N
,

\tilde{f}_{2}(w_{c}+\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-4/3}\zeta N^{-1/3};t_{2})=\mathrm{f}_{2}(\zeta;\mathrm{t}_{2})\cdot\left(1+\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})\right).

(3.30)

Inserting the above estimates, we have

\frac{\tilde{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};t_{1})\tilde{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};t_{2})}{\tilde{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};t_{1})\tilde{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)};t_{2})}=\frac{\mathrm{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};\mathrm{t}_{1})\mathrm{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\mathrm{t}_{2})}{\mathrm{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};\mathrm{t}_{1})\mathrm{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)};\mathrm{t}_{2})}\left(1+c_{1}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})\right),

(3.31)

where
c_{1}=4
and we used the inequality

\left|\prod_{i=1}^{n}(1+x_{i})-1\right|\leq(1+x)^{n}-1\leq 2^{n}x

(3.32)

for all
x_{1},\cdots,x_{n}\in\mathbb{C}
and
x>0
satisfying
|x_{i}|\leq x<1
.

Now we consider the term
\tilde{H}
. Recall the formulas of
H
in (
1.10
) and
\mathrm{S}_{\ell}
in (
1.26
). We have

\begin{split}&\tilde{H}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})=\alpha^{-2/3}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{10/3}N^{4/3}H(U^{(1)},U^{(2)};V^{(1)},V^{(2)})\\
&=\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{-2}\left(\mathrm{S}_{1}^{2}-\mathrm{S}_{2}\right)N^{2/3}+\epsilon^{-3}N\mathrm{S}_{1}+\left(\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{-2}(\mathrm{S}_{1}^{2}+\mathrm{S}_{2})N^{2/3}-\epsilon^{-3}N\mathrm{S}_{1}\right)\cdot\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\frac{v_{i_{1}}^{(1)}}{u_{i_{1}}^{(1)}}\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\frac{u_{i_{2}}^{(2)}}{v_{i_{2}}^{(2)}},\end{split}

(3.33)

where
\epsilon:=\alpha^{1/6}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-1/3}
. Note the following estimate

\begin{split}\frac{w_{c}+(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{-1}\epsilon\zeta N^{-1/3}}{w_{c}}&=\exp\left(-\epsilon N^{-1/3}\zeta-\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}N^{-2/3}\zeta^{2}-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{3}N^{-1}\zeta^{3}+\mathcal{O}(N^{-4/3}(\log N)^{4})\right)\\
&=\exp\left(-\epsilon N^{-1/3}\zeta-\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}N^{-2/3}\zeta^{2}-\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{3}N^{-1}\zeta^{3}\right)\left(1+\mathcal{O}(N^{-4/3}(\log N)^{4})\right)\end{split}

for all
|\zeta|\leq\log N
, where
\mathcal{O}(N^{-4/3}(\log N)^{4})
is uniformly on
\zeta
. Using the inequality (
3.32
), we obtain

\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\frac{v_{i_{1}}^{(1)}}{u_{i_{1}}^{(1)}}\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\frac{u_{i_{2}}^{(2)}}{v_{i_{2}}^{(2)}}=\exp\left(\epsilon N^{-1/3}\mathrm{S}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}N^{-2/3}\mathrm{S}_{2}+\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{3}N^{-1}\mathrm{S}_{3}\right)\left(1+c_{1}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-4/3}(\log N)^{4})\right).

(3.34)

Note the trivial bound
|\mathrm{S}_{\ell}|\leq(k_{1}+k_{2})(\log N)^{\ell}
. We have

\begin{split}\left|\exp\left(\epsilon N^{-1/3}\mathrm{S}_{1}\right)-\sum_{n\leq 3}\frac{1}{n!}(\epsilon N^{-1/3}\mathrm{S}_{1})^{n}\right|&\leq\sum_{n\geq 4}\frac{1}{n!}(\epsilon(k_{1}+k_{2})N^{-1/3}\log N)^{n}\\
&\leq(N^{-1/3}\log N)^{4}\sum_{n\geq 4}\frac{1}{n!}(\epsilon(k_{1}+k_{2}))^{n}\\
&\leq c_{2}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}(N^{-1/3}\log N)^{4},\end{split}

where
c_{2}=e^{\epsilon}
. Thus

\exp\left(\epsilon N^{-1/3}\mathrm{S}_{1}\right)=1+\epsilon N^{-1/3}\mathrm{S}_{1}+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}N^{-2/3}\mathrm{S}_{1}^{2}+\frac{1}{6}\epsilon^{3}N^{-1}\mathrm{S}_{1}^{3}+c_{2}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-4/3}(\log N)^{4}).

Similarly we have

\begin{split}\exp\left(\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}N^{-2/3}\mathrm{S}_{2}\right)&=1+\frac{1}{2}\epsilon^{2}N^{-2/3}\mathrm{S}_{2}+c_{3}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-4/3}(\log N)^{4}),\\
\exp\left(\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{3}N^{-1}\mathrm{S}_{3}\right)&=1+\frac{1}{3}\epsilon^{3}N^{-1}\mathrm{S}_{3}+c_{4}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}\left(N^{-2}(\log N)^{6}\right)\end{split}

for some positive constants
c_{3}
and
c_{4}
.
Inserting the above equations to (
3.34
), and then combining (
3.34
) and (
3.33
), we obtain, after a careful calculation,

\begin{split}\tilde{H}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})&=\frac{1}{12}\mathrm{S}_{1}^{4}+\frac{1}{4}\mathrm{S}_{2}^{2}-\frac{1}{3}\mathrm{S}_{1}\mathrm{S}_{3}+c_{5}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{5})\\
&\ =\mathrm{H}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})+c_{5}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{5})\end{split}

(3.35)

for some positive constant
c_{5}
.

Now we insert (
3.31
) and (
3.35
) into (
3.26
), and obtain

\begin{split}&\alpha^{-1/3}(1+\sqrt{\alpha})^{2/3}N^{2/3}\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)-\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(N)}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{t}_{1},\mathrm{t}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)\\
&=c_{1}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{4})E_{1}+c_{5}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-1/3}(\log N)^{5})E_{2}+(c_{1}c_{5})^{k_{1}+k_{2}}\mathcal{O}(N^{-2/3}(\log N)^{9})E_{2},\end{split}

(3.36)

where

\begin{split}E_{j}&=\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\xi^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\xi^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\left(\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\eta^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}-\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\eta^{(1)}_{i_{1}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{L}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\xi^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\int_{\Gamma^{(N)}_{\mathrm{R}}}\frac{{\mathrm{d}}\eta^{(2)}_{i_{2}}}{2\pi{\mathrm{i}}}\cdot\left(1-\mathrm{z}\right)^{k_{2}}\left(1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right)^{k_{1}}\cdot\frac{\mathrm{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};\mathrm{t}_{1})\mathrm{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\mathrm{t}_{2})}{\mathrm{f}_{1}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};\mathrm{t}_{1})\mathrm{f}_{2}(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)};\mathrm{t}_{2})}\cdot\frac{1}{\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\sum_{i_{\ell}=1}^{k_{\ell}}(\xi_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}-\eta_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)})}\\
&\quad\cdot K_{j}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})\cdot\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\frac{\left(\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}\left(\Delta(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}{\left(\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(\ell)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}\cdot\frac{\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})\Delta(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)})}{\Delta(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)})\Delta(\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})},\end{split}

(3.37)

with

K_{j}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)})=\begin{cases}\mathrm{H}(\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\xi}^{(2)};\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(1)},\boldsymbol{\eta}^{(2)}),&j=1,\\
1,&j=2.\end{cases}

(3.38)

Note that these
E_{j}
terms have similar structure with
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{s}_{1},\mathrm{s}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)
, except that the integration contours
\Gamma_{*}^{(N)}
are subsets of
\Gamma_{*}
appearing in the definition of
\mathrm{\hat{T}}_{k_{1},k_{2}}(\mathrm{z};\mathrm{s}_{1},\mathrm{s}_{2},\mathrm{x};\gamma)
. Recall (
3.15
) in the proof of Lemma
3.3
. It is obvious that we have the same upper bound if we use contours
\Gamma_{*}^{(N)}
instead of
\Gamma_{*}
. Thus we obtain

|E_{1}|\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2})/2}\mathcal{C}_{1}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}.

Similarly we have, by removing the factor
\mathrm{g_{1}}^{4}
, which comes from the estimate of
\mathrm{H}
, in the inequality (
3.15
),

|E_{2}|\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2})/2}(\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{1})^{k_{1}+k_{2}},

where
\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{1}\leq\mathcal{C}_{1}
is a positive constant satisfying the conditions described in Notation
3.2
. Combining the estimates of
|E_{j}|
with (
3.36
), we obtain Lemma
3.5
.

3.2.3
Proof of Lemma
3.6

The proofs for the two estimates are similar, hence we only prove the estimate for
\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)
.

Recall (
3.24
). We have

\begin{split}&\left|\hat{T}_{k_{1},k_{2}}^{(\mathrm{err})}(\mathrm{z};t_{1},t_{2};m,n,M,N)\right|\\
&\leq\sum_{\Delta}\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\left|\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}+\left|\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}\right)\left(\left|\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}+\left|\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|}{2\pi}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|}{2\pi}\cdot\left|1-\mathrm{z}\right|^{k_{2}}\left|1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right|^{k_{1}}\cdot\left|\frac{f_{1}(U^{(1)};t_{1})f_{2}(U^{(2)};t_{2})}{f_{1}(V^{(1)};t_{1})f_{2}(V^{(2)};t_{2})}\right|\cdot\frac{1}{\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left|\sum_{i_{\ell}=1}^{k_{\ell}}(u_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}-v_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)})\right|}\\
&\quad\cdot\left|H(U^{(1)},U^{(2)};V^{(1)},V^{(2)})\right|\cdot\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left|\frac{\left(\Delta(U^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}\left(\Delta(V^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}{\left(\Delta(U^{(\ell)};V^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}\right|\cdot\left|\frac{\Delta(U^{(1)};V^{(2)})\Delta(V^{(1)};U^{(2)})}{\Delta(U^{(1)};U^{(2)})\Delta(V^{(1)};V^{(2)})}\right|.\end{split}

(3.39)

Recall the the sum is over all possible
2^{4k_{1}+2k_{2}}-1
combinations of the contours, except for the only one combination that all the contours are of the form
\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}
(i.e., near the critical point
w_{c}
). Also recall that
\Sigma_{*}=\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}\cup\Sigma_{*}^{(\mathrm{err})}
. The right hand side of (
3.39
) can be rewritten as

\begin{split}&\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|\\
&-\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|,\end{split}

(3.40)

where we suppressed the factors and the integrand for simplifications since they do not affect our argument here. Note the following simple inequality

\prod_{i}(a_{i}+b_{i})-\prod_{i}a_{i}\leq\sum_{\ell}b_{\ell}\prod_{i\neq\ell}(a_{i}+b_{i})

for all nonnegative numbers
a_{i},b_{i}
. We apply this inequality for
a_{i}=\int_{\Sigma_{*}^{(N)}}
and
b_{i}=\int_{\Sigma_{*}^{(\mathrm{err})}}
in (
3.40
). We find that (
3.40
) can be bounded by

\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\delta_{j_{1};1}+\delta_{j_{1};2}+\delta_{j_{1};3}+\delta_{j_{1};4}\right)+\sum_{j_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\left(\delta_{j_{2};5}+\delta_{j_{2};6}\right).

(3.41)

The quantities
\delta_{j,i}
in the above equation are given by

\begin{split}\delta_{j_{1};1}&=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{j_{1}}|\prod_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\cdots\\
\delta_{j_{1};2}&=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{j_{1}}|\prod_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\cdots\\
\delta_{j_{1};3}&=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{j_{1}}|\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\prod_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\cdots\\
\delta_{j_{1};4}&=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{j_{1}}|\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\prod_{i_{1}\neq j_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\cdots\end{split}

where
\cdots
stands for
\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|
, and

\begin{split}\delta_{j_{2};5}&=\cdots\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{j_{2}}|\prod_{i_{2}\neq j_{2}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|,\\
\delta_{j_{2};6}&=\cdots\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{j_{2}}|\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|\prod_{i_{2}\neq j_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}^{(N)}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|,\end{split}

where
\cdots
stands for
\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|\right)
. Here we suppressed the factors and integrands in
\delta_{j;\ell}
for simplifications: They are the same as in (
3.39
).

We have the following estimates:

\delta_{j_{1};\ell}\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2}+4)/2}\mathcal{C}_{2,3}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}Ne^{-c(\ln N)^{3}},\quad 1\leq\ell\leq 4,\ 1\leq j_{1}\leq k_{1},

(3.42)

and

\delta_{j_{2};\ell}\leq k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2}+4)/2}\mathcal{C}_{2,3}^{k_{1}+k_{2}}Ne^{-c(\ln N)^{3}},\quad 5\leq\ell\leq 6,\ 1\leq j_{2}\leq k_{2},

(3.43)

for all
k_{1},k_{2}\geq 1
and sufficiently large
N
, where
\mathcal{C}_{2,3}
is a constant satisfying the conditions described in Notation
3.2
, and
c>0
is a constant appearing in (
3.21
) and (
3.22
). With these estimates, and noting that
(k_{1}+k_{2})^{3}\leq e^{2(k_{1}+k_{2})}
for all
k_{1},k_{2}\geq 0
and that
Ne^{-c(\ln N)^{3}}\ll e^{-c(\ln N)^{3}/2}
for sufficiently large
N
, we obtain Lemma
3.6
immediately.

It remains to show (
3.42
) and (
3.43
). We only prove one representative inequality due to their similarity. Below we show (
3.42
) for
j_{1}=\ell=1
.

We write down the full expression of
\delta_{1;1}
,

\begin{split}&\delta_{1;1}=\left|\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{1}|}{2\pi}\cdot\prod_{i_{1}=2}^{k_{1}}\left(\left|\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}+\left|\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\prod_{i_{1}=1}^{k_{1}}\left(\left|\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}+\left|\frac{\mathrm{z}}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}^{(\Delta)}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v^{(1)}_{i_{1}}|}{2\pi}\right)\cdot\prod_{i_{2}=1}^{k_{2}}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|}{2\pi}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v^{(2)}_{i_{2}}|}{2\pi}\\
&\quad\cdot\left|1-\mathrm{z}\right|^{k_{2}}\left|1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right|^{k_{1}}\cdot\left|\frac{f_{1}(U^{(1)};t_{1})f_{2}(U^{(2)};t_{2})}{f_{1}(V^{(1)};t_{1})f_{2}(V^{(2)};t_{2})}\right|\cdot\frac{1}{\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left|\sum_{i_{\ell}=1}^{k_{\ell}}(u_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}-v_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)})\right|}\\
&\quad\cdot\left|H(U^{(1)},U^{(2)};V^{(1)},V^{(2)})\right|\cdot\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left|\frac{\left(\Delta(U^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}\left(\Delta(V^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}{\left(\Delta(U^{(\ell)};V^{(\ell)})\right)^{2}}\right|\cdot\left|\frac{\Delta(U^{(1)};V^{(2)})\Delta(V^{(1)};U^{(2)})}{\Delta(U^{(1)};U^{(2)})\Delta(V^{(1)};V^{(2)})}\right|.\end{split}

(3.44)

Note that, due to the assumptions of the contours,

\frac{1}{\prod_{\ell=1}^{2}\left|\sum_{i_{\ell}=1}^{k_{\ell}}(u_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)}-v_{i_{\ell}}^{(\ell)})\right|}\leq\frac{1}{|\mathrm{Re}(u_{1}^{(1)}-w_{c})|}\cdot\frac{1}{|\mathrm{Re}(u_{1}^{(2)}-w_{c})|}.

We also use a looser bound for
H
, using the facts that all the contours are bounded and away from
0
,

\left|H(U^{(1)},U^{(2)};V^{(1)},V^{(2)})\right|\leq C\cdot(k_{1}+k_{2})^{2}

for all
k_{1},k_{2}\geq 1
, where
C
is positive constant independent of
k_{1},k_{2}
and all the parameters. Now we use a similar argument as in Section
3.1
and obtain

\begin{split}\delta_{1;1}&\leq C\cdot k_{1}^{k_{1}/2}k_{2}^{k_{2}/2}(k_{1}+k_{2})^{(k_{1}+k_{2}+4)/2}\left(\left|1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right|\theta_{1}+\left|1-\mathrm{z}\right|\theta_{2}\right)^{k_{1}+k_{2}-2}\theta_{3}\cdot|1-\mathrm{z}|\cdot\left|1-\frac{1}{\mathrm{z}}\right|\\
&\quad\cdot\left|\frac{1}{1-\mathrm{z}}\right|\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{1}|}{2\pi}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u_{1}^{(1)};t_{1})|}{\text{dist}(u_{1}^{(1)})\cdot|\mathrm{Re}(u_{1}^{(1)}-w_{c})|}\cdot\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{2}(u^{(2)}_{1};t_{2})||\mathrm{d}u_{1}^{(2)}|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u_{1}^{(2)})\cdot|\mathrm{Re}(u_{1}^{(2)}-w_{c})|},\end{split}

(3.45)

where
\tilde{f}_{\ell}(w;t_{\ell})=f_{\ell}(w;t_{\ell})/f(w_{c};t_{\ell})
as introduced in (
3.27
), and
\theta_{i}
’s are given by

\begin{split}\theta_{1}&=\left(\frac{1}{|1-\mathrm{z}|}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u;t_{1})||\mathrm{d}u|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u)}+\frac{\mathrm{|z|}}{|1-\mathrm{z}|}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u;t_{1})||\mathrm{d}u|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u)}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\left(\frac{1}{|1-\mathrm{z}|}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v|}{2\pi\cdot|\tilde{f}_{1}(v;t_{1})|\cdot\text{dist}(v)}+\frac{\mathrm{|z|}}{|1-\mathrm{z}|}\int_{\Gamma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v|}{2\pi\cdot|\tilde{f}_{1}(v;t_{1})|\cdot\text{dist}(v)}\right),\\
\theta_{2}&=\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{2}(u;t_{2})||\mathrm{d}u|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u)}\right)\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v|}{2\pi\cdot|\tilde{f}_{2}(v;t_{2})|\cdot\text{dist}(v)}\right),\\
\theta_{3}&=\left(\frac{1}{|1-\mathrm{z}|}\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v|}{2\pi\cdot|\tilde{f}_{1}(v;t_{1})|\cdot\text{dist}(v)}+\frac{\mathrm{|z|}}{|1-\mathrm{z}|}\int_{\Gamma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v|}{2\pi\cdot|\tilde{f}_{1}(v;t_{1})|\cdot\text{dist}(v)}\right)\\
&\quad\cdot\left(\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}v|}{2\pi\cdot|\tilde{f}_{2}(v;t_{2})|\cdot\text{dist}(v)}\right),\end{split}

and
\text{dist}(w)
, for
w\in\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}
, is the distance between
w
and the contours
\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{out}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{in}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{R}}\cup\Sigma_{\mathrm{R},\mathrm{out}}
except for the one
w
belongs to. This
\text{dist}(w)
has a similar definition as
\text{dist}(\zeta)
in Section
3.1
but with different contours.

We claim that all of the integrals appearing in
\theta_{i}
values are bounded by some constant
\mathcal{C}_{2;3}^{\prime}
satisfying the conditions described in Notation
3.2
. For example, consider the first integral in
\theta_{1}
,

\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u;t_{1})||\mathrm{d}u|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u)}=\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u;t_{1})||\mathrm{d}u|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u)}+\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u;t_{1})||\mathrm{d}u|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u)},

where the first term is approximately, using (
3.29
),

C^{\prime}\cdot\int_{\Gamma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(N)}}\frac{|\mathrm{f}_{1}(\xi;\mathrm{t}_{1})||\mathrm{d}\xi|}{\text{dist}(\xi)}\leq C^{\prime}\cdot\int_{\Gamma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}}\frac{|\mathrm{f}_{1}(\xi;\mathrm{t}_{1})||\mathrm{d}\xi|}{\text{dist}(\xi)}

for some constant
C^{\prime}
,
and the second term is bounded above by, using (
3.21
),

C^{\prime\prime}\cdot N^{1/3}\cdot e^{-c(\ln N)^{3}}

for some constant
C^{\prime\prime}
, where the extra
N^{1/3}
comes from a possible large factor
1/\text{dist}(u)
. These two estimates confirm the claim for the first factor. Similarly we have the claims for other factors. Thus we have

\theta_{1},\theta_{2},\theta_{3}\leq\mathcal{C}^{\prime}_{2,3}.

Using the similar estimates, we can also obtain

\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L},\mathrm{in}}^{(\mathrm{err})}}\frac{|\mathrm{d}u^{(1)}_{1}|}{2\pi}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{1}(u_{1}^{(1)};t_{1})|}{\text{dist}(u_{1}^{(1)})\cdot|\mathrm{Re}(u_{1}^{(1)}-w_{c})|}\leq C^{\prime\prime\prime}N^{2/3}e^{-c(\ln N)^{3}}

and

\int_{\Sigma_{\mathrm{L}}}\frac{|\tilde{f}_{2}(u^{(2)}_{1};t_{2})||\mathrm{d}u_{1}^{(2)}|}{2\pi\cdot\text{dist}(u_{1}^{(2)})\cdot|\mathrm{Re}(u_{1}^{(2)}-w_{c})|}\leq C^{\prime\prime\prime}N^{1/3}\mathcal{C}^{\prime\prime}_{2,3},

where the extra
N^{1/3}
comes from a possible large factor
1/|\mathrm{Re}(w-w_{c})|
. Combing all these estimates in (
3.45
), we obtain (
3.42
) for
j_{1}=\ell=1
. Other estimates in (
3.42
) and (
3.43
) are similar.
